<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">یحیی بن سعید سے روایت ہے کہ ایک شخص مر گیا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم کے زمانے میں ایک شخص بولا واہ کیا اچھی موت ہوئی نہ کچھ بیماری ہوئی نہ کچھ آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے فرمایا بھلا یہ کیا کہتا ہے تجھے کیا معلوم ہے کہ اگر جل جلالہ اس کو کسی بیماری میں مبتلا کرتا تو اس کے گناہوں کو معاف کرتا ہے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1718]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1718" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya bin Saeed se riwayat hai ke ek shakhs mar gaya. Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke zamane mein ek shakhs bola wah kya achi maut hui na kuch bimari hui na kuch. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya bhala ye kya kehta hai? Tujhe kya maloom hai ke agar Jalla Jalaluhu isko kisi bimari mein mubtala karta to uske gunahon ko maaf karta.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1718]</p>

## Narration 1698

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1719)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 50</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَزِيدَ بْنِ خُصَيْفَةَ، أَنَّ عَمْرَو بْنَ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ كَعْبٍ السَّلَمِيَّ، أَخْبَرَهُ أَنَّ نَافِعَ بْنَ جُبَيْرٍ أَخْبَرَهُ عَنْ عُثْمَانَ بْنِ أَبِي الْعَاصِ، أَنَّهُ أَتَى رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ عُثْمَانُ وَبِي وَجَعٌ قَدْ كَادَ يُهْلِكُنِي ‏.‏ قَالَ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ امْسَحْهُ بِيَمِينِكَ سَبْعَ مَرَّاتٍ وَقُلْ أَعُوذُ بِعِزَّةِ اللَّهِ وَقُدْرَتِهِ مِنْ شَرِّ مَا أَجِدُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ فَقُلْتُ ذَلِكَ فَأَذْهَبَ اللَّهُ مَا كَانَ بِي فَلَمْ أَزَلْ آمُرُ بِهَا أَهْلِي وَغَيْرَهُمْ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1719]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1719" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1719</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1719 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yazid ibn Khusayfa that Amr ibn Abdullah ibn Kab as-Salami told him that Nafi ibn Jubayr told him that Uthman ibn Abi al-As came to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace. Uthman said that he had a pain which was enough to kill him. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Rub it with your right hand seven times and say, &#x27;I take refuge with the might of Allah and His power from the evil of what I feel.&#x27; &quot; Uthman added, &quot;I said that, and Allah removed what I had. I still command my family and others to say it</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1719]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1719" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1719</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1719 ]</p>

</details>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">उस्मान बिन अबी आस (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के वो रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के पास आये, उनके ऐसा दर्द होता था जिससे करीब-ए-हलाकत के थे। आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया दहिना हाथ अपने दर्द के मकाम पर सात बार फेर और कह &quot;अऊजु बिइज़्ज़तिल्लाहि व कुदरतिही मिन शर्री मा अजिदु&quot;। उस्मान कहते हैं मैंने यही कहा अल्लाह ने मेरा दर्द दूर कर दिया, फिर मैं हमेशा अपने घर वालों को और दूसरे लोगों को इसका हुकुम दिया करता।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1719]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1719" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عثمان بن ابی عاص رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ وہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم کے پاس آئے ان کے ایسا درد ہوتا تھا جس سے قریب ہلاکت کے تھے آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے فرمایا داہنا ہاتھ اپنے درد کے مقام پر سات بار پھیر اور کہہ &quot; اعوذ بعزۃ اللہ وقدرتہ من شر ما اجد &quot; عثمان کہتے ہیں میں نے یہی کہا اللہ نے میرا درد دور کر دیا پھر میں ہمیشہ اپنے گھر والوں کو اور دوسرے لوگوں کو اس کا حکم دیا کرتا ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1719]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1719" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Usman bin Abi Aas (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke wo Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke paas aaye, unke aisa dard hota tha jisse qareeb-e-halakat ke thay. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya dahina haath apne dard ke maqam par saat baar pher aur keh &quot;A&#x27;uzu bi&#x27;izzatillahi wa qudratihi min sharri ma ajidu&quot;. Usman kehte hain maine yahi kaha Allah ne mera dard door kar diya, phir main hamesha apne ghar walon ko aur dusre logon ko iska hukum diya karta.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1719]</p>

## Narration 1699

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1720)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 50</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ عُرْوَةَ بْنِ الزُّبَيْرِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم كَانَ إِذَا اشْتَكَى يَقْرَأُ عَلَى نَفْسِهِ بِالْمُعَوِّذَاتِ وَيَنْفِثُ ‏.‏ قَالَتْ فَلَمَّا اشْتَدَّ وَجَعُهُ كُنْتُ أَنَا أَقْرَأُ عَلَيْهِ وَأَمْسَحُ عَلَيْهِ بِيَمِينِهِ رَجَاءَ بَرَكَتِهَا ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1720]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1720" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Urwa ibn az- Zubayr from A&#x27;isha that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, when he had a complaint, would recite the last three suras of Qur&#x27;an, over himself and blow. She said, &quot;When his pain was great, I would recite it over him and wipe him with his right hand hoping for its blessing</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1720]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1720" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हज़रात उम्मुल मोमिनीन आयेशा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) जब बीमार होते तो कुल हुवल्लाहु आहद और कुल आ&#x27;उज़ू बी रब्बिल फ़लक़ और कुल आ&#x27;उज़ू बी राबिन नस पढ़ कर अपने ऊपर फूँकते. हज़रात आयेशा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) ने कहा के जब आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) बीमार हुए तो मैं इन सूरतों को पढ़ कर आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) का दाहिना हाथ आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के जिस्म-इ-मुबारक पर फेरती बरकत के वास्ते.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1720]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1720" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حضرت ام المومنین عائشہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہا سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم جب بیمار ہوتے تو قل ہو اللہ احدا اور قل اعوذ برب الفلق اور قل اعوذبرب الناس پڑھ کر اپنے اوپر پھونکتے حضرت عائشہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہا نے کہا کہ جب آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم بیمار ہوئے تو میں ان سورتوں کو پڑھ کر آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم کا داہنا ہاتھ آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم کے جسم مبارک پر پھیرتی برکت کے واسطے</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1720]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1720" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Hazrat Ummul Momineen Ayesha (Radi Allahu Anha) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) jab bimar hote to Qul Huwallahu Ahad aur Qul A&#x27;uzu bi Rabbil Falaq aur Qul A&#x27;uzu bi Rabbin Nas padh kar apne upar phoonkte. Hazrat Ayesha (Radi Allahu Anha) ne kaha ke jab aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) bimar hue to main in suraton ko padh kar aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ka dahina haath aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke jism-e-mubarak par pherti barkat ke waste.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1720]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ عَمْرَةَ بِنْتِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، أَنَّ أَبَا بَكْرٍ الصِّدِّيقَ، دَخَلَ عَلَى عَائِشَةَ وَهِيَ تَشْتَكِي وَيَهُودِيَّةٌ تَرْقِيهَا فَقَالَ أَبُو بَكْرٍ ارْقِيهَا بِكِتَابِ اللَّهِ ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">This reading preserves separate source spans. Intervening source notes are retained under Comments. Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abu Bakr as-Siddiq (companion). The source reports Abu Bakr&#x27;s visit and instruction. Separate explicit bracketed and parenthetical translation glosses while keeping the four passages as one connected Companion report. Some reports contain separate source passages. Intervening source text is retained under Comments.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Said from Amra bint Abd ar-Rahman that Abu Bakr as-Siddiq visited A&#x27;isha while she had a</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Intervening source text is retained under Comments.</p>

<p dir="ltr"> complaint and a jewish woman was making incantation</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Intervening source text is retained under Comments.</p>

<p dir="ltr"> for her. Abu Bakr said, &quot;Do it</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अमरा बिंत अब्दुर रहमान (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हा) से रिवायत है के अबू बक्र (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) हज़रत आयशा (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हा) के पास आये, वो बीमार थीं और एक यहूदी औरत उन पर पढ़ कर फूँक रही थी। हज़रत अबू बक्र (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा: कलाम-ए-अल्लाह (कुरआन) पढ़ कर फूँक।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1721" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Amra bint Abdur Rahman (Radi Allahu Anha) se riwayat hai ke Abu Bakr (Radi Allahu Anhu) Hazrat Ayesha (Radi Allahu Anha) ke paas aaye, woh bimar thin aur ek Yahudi aurat un par parh kar phoonk rahi thi. Hazrat Abu Bakr (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha: Kalam-e-Allah (Quran) parh kar phoonk.</p>
